[Palace, grounds]
Bright flowers flourish under the trees which line the sanded path leading
from the gate to the formal entrance of the Royal Palace. One could spend
hours admiring the work of the Royal Gardener who tends these grounds. The
palace itself stands witness to another artisan at the top of his profession.
Broad steps of blue marble lead to a series of fluted marble pillars, which
with their entablature, are sculpted to look like nymphs. Ivy in urns between
the pillars embrace the nymphs in chaste leaves. A functionary pauses in the
entryway, tapping his finger against a tablet in his hands, then continues on.
A gate leads back to the city.
You see Frederick. 
Obvious exits: east, west.
> look w
[Palace, pool]
Slender, vigorous elms embrace a man-made tide pool on three sides, while the
fourth opens into a beach. A Royal Game Warden watches over its multitude of
marine life forms to ensure the health of the fragile ecosystem. Still, the
pool invites you to cool off in its crystal depths or to enjoy carefree hours
of fishing.

a presence as the fountain.

        ********************************************
        *        The Andoran Chronicle             *
        ********************************************
        

           COSTUME PARTY!  COME ONE COME ALL!

Taylor Quicksilver and Garin Stonehand invite you to their home for an evening
of revels and festivities!  

Food and drink will be provided of course!  Enjoy swimming in the calm waters
off our stretch of beach, or do some dancing... well, we won't say where, but
we're pretty sure you'll find it an experience worth remembering!

Wear a costume!  Prizes will be awarded for the best, the most imaginative, the
most humerous!

A costumer will be available before the party to help you with your costuming
needs.
The date is set for October 10th at 10pm EST.


[OOC:  Everyone is invited!  Please leave your IC (and OOC) arguments and
enmities at the door... such goings on at our place will not be welcome! :)]

You gaze upon your own reflection...
You see Chandra McFearless, a petite female wolfing teenager with long
blonde hair with curls.  Bright blue eyes gaze out at the world in
wonderment and sparkle with the call of the wild.
She is wearing some exquisite blue diamond earrings encased in a thin sheath of
pure white gold polished to a high sheen, a pink fur hat, some delicate white
lace gloves lined with palest pink silk, a delicate white alzyron necklace with
a shimmering rose pendant cut from a flawless white diamond, a sterling silver
pin shaped like a wolf with topaz yellow eyes, some shadow fur gloves, an
ancient gleaming white mask, a white gold medallion embossed with the
McFearless family walking in the peaceful forest of Grymwood, some pink
sneakers with a wave emblazoned on the tongues, some black framed violet
sunglasses with silver starbursts on each lens, some shadow fur socks, a dark
crystal pendant, some eldritch leather armor, a delicate white gold bracelet
engraved with a beautiful butterfly inlaid with four perfect sapphires for
wings and a pink ribbon.
She is wielding a dented ebony main-gauche emblazoned with a leering skull and
an enchanted dagger.
She radiates with magical strength.
She appears to be healthy.
She has a lacerated, fractured and burnt head, a lacerated and singed body, a
nicked, smashed and slightly punctured back, a bludgeoned and singed right arm,
a nicked, fractured and scorched right leg and a singed right hand.

new equipment can be afforded. Main Street continues west behind the stalls.

> look zhar
Standing a mere two feet tall this horrid monstrosity created in the
infernal pits of Nygal'Ag inspires fear in all who gaze upon it. Made
from collected body parts, the Zharkier has been given life by foul
magic. Each individual part that makes up the creature has had the
skin flayed from it, leaving only the bloody organ or muscle itself
to pulse evilly in the light. A putrescent, sickly liquid forms a
protective coating around the organs and also drips continously from
the creature, creating small piles of a foul-smelling pus wherever it
walks. A sinewy spinal column, glowing with fiery red nerve endings
serves as the creatures tail, while a gaping hole in the creature's
throat emits glowing pustules of blood as the monster roars. A small
diamond pendant with a ruby in the center is imbedded into its chest,
and pulses occasionaly.
It is wielding a shadowy fragment.
It radiates with magical strength.
It is surrounded by a prismatic globe.
It is outlined in glowing armor.
It is protected by a ghostly, hovering shield.
It is the center of a large, white sphere of light.

[Inner Sea, break waters]

Wide-winged hawks fly long circles below you, alert for any movement of their

the trail now would be more than usually dangerous.

[Wolfling Den]
This large, roomy area has walls with old cracks covered over with
soft furs. A large, silver-lined basin of water flowing into it 
rests in a back corner. A fire pit sits near a grouping of stones
for sitting. A lone wolf stands guard at the entrance, his eyes 
battle wary. Near the back of the dens, an excavation has begun to
clear out the individual rooms of the pack. The fresh smell of spring
flowers fills the air with every breath.
You see Wolfbane, Cypress, Khalil, Sun, Mariella, Rion, Cy'ry, Graul, Feras and
Saxon.

!> look khal
Before you stands a rugged male wolfling, Khalil. He is well tanned and toned
from his time in the wilds. His deep gray eyes in contrast to his chiseled
physique and unkempt gray hair show compassion and a fire for life.
He is wearing some archer's titanium armor with the image of a wolfling archer
hunting the forests of Grymwood, a gray leather sheath, a glowing albescent pin
carved in the shape of a spruce pine, a silver ring, a forest green cloak
embroidered with the emblem of a large silver oak, a rattlesnake skin belt, a
mirmach leaf medallion, some black doeskin boots and a darkened wood ring worn
smooth from the passing of years.
Unable to hide it any longer, his weariness is apparent.
He is protected by a ghostly, hovering shield.
A glistening blue glow surrounds him.
A dull orange glow emanates from him.
He has distinctly feline eyes.
He appears to be healthy.
He has a lacerated, grazed and scorched head, a nicked and grazed body, a
lacerated, slightly punctured and scorched back, a nicked right arm, a nicked
and singed left arm, a lacerated, banged-up and grazed left leg, a nicked and
singed right hand and a nicked, slightly punctured and singed left hand.

13) The people of the new land, after fighting for their rightful place in 
time, shall find themselves handed to the great empire. Their days of peace 
shall be limited and death will once again walk with them.

!> read book
(Written by Father Quinn) The numbering of the prophecies has nothing to do
with their order, and I have tried to group them together by the ones that
are similar in nature. Again, the numbering has nothing to do with order at
all, it is simply a means to know who is talking about which one.

1) Though planted, the seed shall not grow without the nectar blessed by 
Coryssa.
